## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 我是 Ducter，你的个人 AI 搭档。比如你可以让我修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your personal AI partner. For example, I can fix a failing test in one of your repos while you do something else.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요! 저는 Ducter예요. 이 컴퓨터에서 실제 작업을 맡아 처리할 수 있어요. 예를 들어 "이 저장소에서 깨진 테스트 고쳐줘"처럼 말씀해 주시면 됩니다.
```

### ok-keeps-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 고마워요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
  [partner message] 좋아요, 내일 아침에 봐요. 편하게 쉬어요.
--- step 2: user says: OK
  [partner message] 네, 내일 아침에 봐요.
```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは「届くまで確実に届ける」、UDPは「速さ優先で、届かなくても気にしない」通信方式です。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para ordenar tus commits locales antes de compartir la rama, así el historial queda lineal y limpio. Usa merge cuando la rama ya está publicada o compartida, porque reescribir commits públicos rompe el trabajo de otros.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠的、有序的字节流：收发双方先建连，丢了的包会重传，数据按顺序交到对面；UDP 不建连、不保证送达和顺序，发出去就不管了，所以快、开销小。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own unpushed feature branch to update it onto the latest base and keep history linear. Merge when the branch is shared or already pushed, since rebasing rewrites commits others may have built on.
```

### group-split-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: ~/dev/ducter 的登录页要重做：先把登录表单换成新的输入框组件，然后接上短信验证码，最后补登录页的端到端测试。一件做完再做下一件。
  [partner ack line] 好，我按顺序做：先换登录表单的输入框，接着接短信验证码，最后补端到端测试，每件做完告诉你。
  [opened a task] title: 登录页重做 1/3：换成新的输入框组件 | group: 登录页重做 | place: {"kind":"repo","device":"local","repoPath":"~/dev/ducter"} | Ducter gave it to: claude-code (opus) | brief: 用户原话：“~/dev/ducter 的登录页要重做：先把登录表单换成新的输入框组件，然后接上短信验证码，最后补登录页的端到端测试。一件做完再做下一件。” ⏎  ⏎ 本任务是第 1 件：把登录页的登录表单换成新的输入框组件。 ⏎ 决定已定：按顺序做，本件只做输入框替换，不接短信验证码，不补端到端测试。 ⏎  ⏎ 完成标准： ⏎ - 登录表单的输入框全部改用仓库里的新输入框组件（先在仓库里找到它；若找不到或有多个候选拿不准，用 needs_input 问我）。 ⏎ - 现有测试和类型检查/构建通过，报告里附上实际运行的命令和结果。 ⏎ - 改动在任务自己的分支上提交。 ⏎  ⏎ 不做的事：不合并到主分支，不推送，不开 PR；不改短信验证码和端到端测试相关的代码；不改与登录页无关的组件。 ⏎  ⏎ 地点：仓库 ~/dev/ducter。
  [held in line by Ducter, opens when the part before it is done] title: 登录页重做 2/3：接上短信验证码 | group: 登录页重做
  [held in line by Ducter, opens when the part before it is done] title: 登录页重做 3/3：补端到端测试 | group: 登录页重做
--- under the user's message: 1. 登录页重做 1/3：换成新的输入框组件 (working); 2. 登录页重做 2/3：接上短信验证码 (working); 3. 登录页重做 3/3：补端到端测试 (working)
```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 直接在 `bun test` 后面加上文件路径就行：

```bash
bun test tests/math.test.ts
```

如果不想写全路径，也可以给文件名的一部分，Bun 会匹配路径里包含这段字符的测试文件：

```bash
bun test math
```

要只跑某个测试用例，用 `-t`（或 `--test-name-pattern`）按名字过滤：

```bash
bun test -t "加法"
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is a file where every change to a database is written down before it's applied to the main data. If the system crashes midway, the database can replay that log on restart to recover anything that was committed but not yet fully saved.
```

--- step 1: user answers the open decision: go
  [partner message] 好，我把这些做成一个看板。
  [partner message] 做好了，周五的 G1、G3 都是 553 元，看板里能看到剩余票数。之后每天的票价会更新到这里。
  [an app card under the message: made "京沪高铁周五票价", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1713 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">600 元以下最低价</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="low">—</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="lowsub">周五北京到上海</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">周五班次</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  </div>
  <p class="k-sub" id="when"></p>
</main>
<script>
function render() {
  var d = ducter.get('board') || {};
  var trains = d.trains || [];
  var under = trains.filter(function (t) { return t.price < 600; });
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  list.innerHTML = '';
  trains.forEach(function (t) {
    var row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'k-row';
    var main = document.createElement('div');
    main.className = 'k-row-main';
    main.textContent = t.no + (t.price < 600 ? '' : '（超过 600）');
    var sub = document.createElement('div');
    sub.className = 'k-row-sub';
    sub.textContent = t.seats || '';
    main.appendChild(sub);
    var val = document.createElement('div');
    val.className = 'k-row-value';
    val.textContent = t.price + ' 元';
    row.appendChild(main);
    row.appendChild(val);
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
  if (under.length) {
    var min = Math.min.apply(null, under.map(function (t) { return t.price; }));
    document.getElementById('low').textContent = min + ' 元';
    document.getElementById('lowsub').textContent = under.length + ' 班在 600 以下';
  } else {
    document.getElementById('low').innerHTML = '<span class="k-none">没有</span>';
    document.getElementById('lowsub').textContent = '今天 600 以下没有班次';
  }
  document.getElementById('when').textContent = d.updated ? '更新：' + d.updated : '';
}
ducter.onChange(render);
render();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"board":{"updated":"第 3 天","trains":[{"no":"G1","price":553,"seats":"二等座余 12 张"},{"no":"G3","price":553,"seats":"二等座余 4 张"},{"no":"G7","price":626,"seats":"二等座"}]}}

  [an app card under the message: made "hawdb nightly write benchmark", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 2455 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <section class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Latest night</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="latest">Waiting for first run</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="latest-sub">Writes per second and p99 latency, updated after each nightly run</div>
  </section>
  <h2 class="k-h">Nights</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="nights"><div class="k-empty">Nightly results will list here, newest first.</div></div>
  </div>
<style>
  .k-app { padding: 16px; background: var(--d-bg); color: var(--d-ink); font-family: inherit; }
  .k-h { font-size: 15px; margin: 18px 0 8px; color: var(--d-soft); }
  .k-card { background: var(--d-card); border: 1px solid var(--d-line); border-radius: 12px; padding: 8px 12px; }
  .k-row { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; gap: 12px; padding: 10px 0; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--d-line); }
  .k-row:last-child { border-bottom: 0; }
  .k-row-main { font-weight: 600; }
  .k-row-sub { color: var(--d-muted); font-size: 13px; margin-top: 2px; }
  .k-row-value { text-align: right; font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; }
  .k-empty { color: var(--d-muted); padding: 12px 0; }
</style>
<script>
  function render(nights) {
    var list = document.getElementById('nights');
    var latest = document.getElementById('latest');
    var sub = document.getElementById('latest-sub');
    if (!nights || !nights.length) return;
    var sorted = nights.slice().reverse();
    var top = sorted[0];
    latest.textContent = top.writes + ' writes/s';
    sub.textContent = top.night + ' · p99 ' + top.p99 + ' · compaction stalls ' + (top.stalls == null ? '—' : top.stalls);
    list.innerHTML = '';
    sorted.forEach(function (n) {
      var row = document.createElement('div');
      row.className = 'k-row';
      row.innerHTML = '<div><div class="k-row-main"></div><div class="k-row-sub"></div></div>' +
        '<div class="k-row-value"><div></div><div class="k-row-sub"></div></div>';
      row.querySelector('.k-row-main').textContent = n.night;
      row.querySelector('.k-row-sub').textContent = 'p99 ' + n.p99 + ' · stalls ' + (n.stalls == null ? '—' : n.stalls);
      row.querySelector('.k-row-value div').textContent = n.writes + ' writes/s';
      list.appendChild(row);
    });
    ducter.summary(top.night + ': ' + top.writes + ' writes/s');
  }
  ducter.onChange(function () { render(ducter.get('nights')); });
  render(ducter.get('nights'));
</script>
</main>; its data now (what it opens with): {"nights":[{"night":"Night 2","writes":"181k","p99":"4.0 ms","stalls":null},{"night":"Night 3","writes":"182k","p99":"4.1 ms","stalls":0}]}

function render(){
  var list = document.getElementById("list");
  list.innerHTML = items.map(function(p, i){
    var sub = [p.status, p.since].filter(Boolean).join(" · ");
    return '<button class="k-row" aria-pressed="' + (p.done ? "true" : "false") + '" data-i="' + i + '" style="width:100%;text-align:left;background:none;border:0;padding:0;cursor:pointer">' +
      '<span class="k-row-main"><span style="' + (p.done ? "text-decoration:line-through;opacity:.5" : "") + '">' +
        '<strong>' + esc(p.id) + '</strong> ' + esc(p.title) + '</span>' +
        '<span class="k-row-sub">' + esc(sub) + '</span></span>' +
      '<span class="k-row-value">' + (p.done ? "✓ landed" : "open") + '</span>' +
    '</button>';
  }).join("");

  var open = items.filter(function(p){ return !p.done; }).length;
  var landed = items.length - open;
  document.getElementById("open").textContent = open;
  document.getElementById("landed").textContent = landed + " landed";
  ducter.summary(open + " open, " + landed + " landed");
}

document.getElementById("list").addEventListener("click", function(e){
  var row = e.target.closest("button[data-i]");
  if(!row) return;
  var i = Number(row.getAttribute("data-i"));
  items[i].done = !items[i].done;
  ducter.set("prs", items);
  render();
});

Promise.resolve(ducter.get("prs")).then(function(saved){
  if(Array.isArray(saved) && saved.length) items = saved;
  render();
});
ducter.onChange(function(){
  Promise.resolve(ducter.get("prs")).then(function(saved){
    if(Array.isArray(saved) && saved.length){ items = saved; render(); }
  });
});
</script>
</main>; its data now (what it opens with): {"default":[{"id":"ducter#412","title":"Compaction cut","status":"Waiting on Weizhen's review","since":"since Monday","done":false},{"id":"mem#10287","title":"Retry on 502","status":"CI is failing","since":"","done":false},{"id":"hawdb#1592","title":"Flaky compaction test","status":"Waiting on Alice","since":"since Tuesday","done":false},{"id":"ducter#418","title":"Apps bar","status":"Draft, not yet waiting","since":"","done":false}]}
  [also used tools] app
```

</div>

<div id="pick"></div>

  <div class="k-field"><label>出差名称</label><input class="k-input" id="trip" value="北京出差"></div>
  <div class="k-field"><label>花销内容</label><input class="k-input" id="what" placeholder="如 高铁 G1"></div>
  <div class="k-field"><label>日期</label><input class="k-input" id="day" placeholder="如 11/20"></div>
  <div class="k-chips" id="cats">
    <button class="k-chip" aria-pressed="true" data-c="交通">交通</button>

  </div>
  <div class="k-field"><label>金额</label><input class="k-num" id="amt" inputmode="decimal"></div>
  <button class="k-btn primary" id="save">保存</button>
</details>

.k-row{cursor:pointer}
.k-row-sub{color:var(--d-muted)}
#pick{margin-top:12px}
</style>
<script>
let cat='交通', sel=-1;
const fmt=n=>'¥'+Number(n||0).toLocaleString('zh-CN');
function draw(){
  const xs=duc … length?('交通 '+fmt(byCat('交通'))+' · 住宿 '+fmt(byCat('住宿'))):'记下第一笔就会算合计';
  ducter.summary(xs.length?(xs.length+' 笔,合计 '+fmt(total)):'还没记');
  const pick=document.getElementById('pick');pick.textContent='';
  if(sel>=0&&xs[sel]){
    const b=document.createElement('button');b.className='k-btn';
    b.textContent='删除这一笔';
    b.onclick=()=>{const ys=ducter.get('items')||[];ys.splice(sel,1);ducter.set('items',ys);sel=-1;draw()};
    pick.appendChild(b);
  }
}
document.querySelectorAll('#cats button').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{
  cat=b.dataset.c;
  document.querySelectorAll('#cats button').forEach(o=>o.setAttribute('aria-pressed',o===b?'true':'false'));
});
document.getElementById('save').onclick=()=>{
  const amt=document.getElementById('amt').value;
  if(amt===''||isNaN(Number(amt)))return;
  const xs=ducter.get('items')||[];
  xs.push({
    trip:document.getElementById('trip').value||'出差',
    what:document.getElementById('what').value,
    day:document.getElementById('day').value,
    cat:cat,
    amt:Number(amt)
  });
  ducter.set('items',xs);
  document.getElementById('amt').value='';
  document.getElementById('what').value='';
  draw();
};
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"trip":"北京出差","what":"高铁 G1 上海虹桥→北京南 07:00","day":"11/20","cat":"交通","amt":662},{"trip":"北京出差","what":"亚朵酒店（望京）两晚","day":"11/20–21","cat":"住宿","amt":1036}]}

适合放在空状态里。它线条简单、颜色温和，不会让人觉得出错了，空白屏幕正好表示“还没有内容”。如果你的空状态是“还没有笔记”“还没有会话”这类场景，它很贴切。

有两点要注意：一是它是通用的笔记本图，如果页面主题很具体（比如账单、健康数据），可能需要换成更相关的图。二是它是浅色的，放进深色模式时要确认在暗底上仍然清楚。
